# Live-check backlog

One ledger of every open question that only the real game (007: Nightfire PS2 NTSC-U retail, running in PCSX2
with the trace tools) can settle. Harvested from every report's "Live checks for the main agent" section in
`docs/reports/*.md` and from the `docs/UNKNOWNS.md` / `docs/GAPS.md` rows whose "how to settle" needs live play
(~60 reports were assumed; the sweep found 204 reports carrying live-check sections — all harvested).

The idea is REA's `record_unknown` / `update_unknown`: an unresolved question stays on the ledger with the
procedure that settles it, the expected answers and what each would change in the port, and the supporting AND
contradicting evidence — until qualifying observed evidence (a live measurement recorded in `docs/live/`, the
merge log, or a later report) resolves it. Reports scatter the same thing across their own "Live checks"
sections where checks get lost; this file is the one place they live.

## Row anatomy

One line per check:

```
<id> | <source> | <type> | <lvl> | Q: <question> | HOW: <procedure> | EXP: <expected answers + impact> | EV: <evidence for/against, citations kept> | ST: <status> | SETTLED: <where the answer lives / why obsolete>
```

- **id**: `L-001`… sequential over the whole ledger (assigned in report-name order, in four harvest blocks:
  L-001–198, L-200–394, L-400–598, L-600–786; the UNKNOWNS/GAPS rows continue at L-801+ with their original
  `U-xxx` / `G-xxx` id kept in the source column). Gaps in the numbering are reserved ranges of the harvest
  blocks, not missing rows.
- **source**: the report stem (filename without `docs/reports/` and `.md`), or `UNKNOWNS`/`GAPS` with the row id.
- **type**: `pcsx2` — needs the real game (tools: `tools/trace/pcsx2_shot.py` for placed screenshots,
  `gdbwatch.py` for read/write watches on an address, `gdbcall.py --spec "…"` to call a game function,
  `fieldprobe.py` to poke values and shoot, `pad_log.py` / `goto_level.py` / `cheats.py` / PINE reads);
  `render` — an nf-view `--bond x,y,z,yaw,pitch` headless spot, no game needed; `both`.
- **lvl**: the level(s) the check runs on (`07000006`, `05`, `MP`, `front-end`, `-` if not level-bound).
- **ST**: `open` · `answered` (SETTLED names the `docs/live/` file or report that records the answer) ·
  `obsolete` (SETTLED says why the question no longer applies) · `settled-in-report` (the source report itself
  recorded the answer).
- Rows are source-faithful: the wording, spots, addresses and citations are the report's own, compressed but
  not corrected. Where later work overturned a report's framing, the row's ST/SETTLED says so; near-duplicate
  rows from different reports are kept until resolution dedupes them.

Answered rows are additionally listed, with their answers, in "Answered" below; the full ledger follows.

## Answered

Checks whose answer is already recorded live. Each row below is the ledger's evidence; mark the matching row
`ST: answered` with `SETTLED:` pointing here. The authoritative files are `docs/live/2026-10-13-portal-ground-truth.md`,
`docs/live/2026-10-13-weapon-modes-pcsx2.md` and `docs/live/2026-10-14-weapon-measurements-pcsx2.md`; the
merge log and WORKLOG record the rest.

- **Portal 06/07 ground truth** (portal-ground-truth): the courtyard's walkway masonry and lower castle wall
  draw solid straight after the jump (a port bug, not a teleport artifact); the 07 ballroom window shows only
  the snowy mountain (the game hides that room — the port's walk matches); the 07 clock hall matches. Also
  settled the VisCelList PINE puzzle (Vision_Portal_Recurse runs 3×/frame; PINE between frames reads the LAST
  viewer's list; VisCelCount u16 @0x30d0bc, VisCelList @0x34b3b0, 8-byte entries), the courtyard's visible
  rooms (2 cels every frame: eye 0x0179f230 depth 0, 0x0179fb30 depth 1), the 40-node chain of 0x0179fb30,
  and that View_AddForcedObjects does NOT draw the courtyard walls (710 objects, 12 pass the gates, none a
  wall — refuting fix-portal4's hypothesis).
- **Weapon fire modes** (weapon-modes-pcsx2): the Sentinel's guided camera (view becomes the rocket ~2 s, red
  tint, ammo pane stays, right stick steers, red static on impact); MGL 0x2A impact vs 0x2B ~3–4 s delay;
  the twin swap needs the twin to have rounds; the full 32-weapon mode table (burst counts 3, AIMS-20→Grenade,
  Scorpion Heatseeker→Unguided, Samurai overcharge/beam); Classic Bond puts Alt. Fire on Square (circle no-op).
- **Weapon measurements** (weapon-measurements-pcsx2): unguided rocket 0.3367 u/frame constant (port's 0.0625
  5.4× slow); frag leaves the hand ≈0.3 s (18–21 frames) after the press; stunner press-activated,
  contact-range (~1 unit), drains ~32/s, target stunned 0x4a→0x4b and disarmed after ~1.5 s; laser watch beam
  from watch face to crosshair, drains ~31/s; remote mine sticks (no bounce), ○ → detonator 0x38, fire blows
  it next frame; AIMS-20 lights lit from the draw, pattern per ammo/mode, ○ swaps only with grenade rounds;
  SG5 casings small, up-left, gone in ~2 frames; P2K silencer = long tube on the barrel; guard sighting→first
  shot ≈ +97 frames (~1.6 s), bursts 50–60 frames apart; killing the 06 truck driver fails the mission at
  once; level loadouts differ per level (14 starts crossbow-in-hand; gadget cycles differ; 05 holds no frags);
  twin records share the base's clip slot; fire action float 0x2a3900; DynamicObjList head 0x270594.
- **Also answered elsewhere** (SETTLED column names the place): the 05 gate-door icon context 0xFF (settled
  fix-swim-vent's scope check; fix-swim-vent5/WORKLOG); the grapple spot's no-prompt pin and the prompt's
  sphere-pick volume ~2.5 (fix-context-hints2, fix-wallhug-prompt); the 08 east-balcony wall jump context
  0xFF (fix-08-walls); the EE FPU rounding model (division rounds-to-nearest, add/sub/mul chop — WORKLOG 0026);
  the AI combat trace ground truth (notice after 61 ticks, 3-shot bursts — WORKLOG 0019); spawner p7 = the
  KILL channel (merge log, refuting fix-actors' arm reading); the bldata live census (regen slot, cursor
  decay, tilt-ignores-zoom); the Raptor drop chain vs the 01-raptor trace; the castle is not mirrored (U-023);
  frame pacing (port 98.8% 60 Hz, PCSX2 55.3 vblanks/s — U-013 partly); drone posing census (13/16 bit-exact,
  U-028); the 07 Kimiko scene compare (every shot matches; letterbox + 4:3; no contact effect on character
  hits — 108a/108d); the front-end walk (101k) that rebuilt the menus; single-player has NO radar; the 05
  trench eye cel is 15 (build_FindCel tie-break, fixed); the 0c pylon draws (real-hardware pass); the Frinesi
  shell eject; the 109u HUD viewfinder redo; swim entry at the game's depth; laser/stunner fire script holds;
  the AIMS light datums 8..0xB; the armour ring (the captures' orange 007 ring is the armour state).
- Per-row answered statuses are marked inline in the ledger (`ST: answered`) where the match is certain.

## Obsolete

Questions that no longer apply (the framing died, the task was superseded, or the observation was an artifact).
Kept here so nobody re-opens them: fix-portal4's forced-objects wall theory (refuted live); fix-ai-traces'
"script-less" truck driver ([L] 05-truck: state 0x32 for 700 frames); fix-actors' spawner p7 arm reading (p7
is the kill channel); the atlas's VisCelList puzzle framing; the remote mine "→0x39" detonator row (it is
0x38); "Square is the mode button" (save layout: ○ mode, □ reload, L2 cycles; the port key is B); fix-swim-vent4's
"icon_ctx 6 at the door" stand-in (the game reads 0xFF there); the flat 6.0 shimmy-prompt window (sphere pick
~2.5); fix-exact-sine-flip's live check (the flip does not reproduce on master); exact-math-swap2's "camera
shift" (the comparer's own sbs.ps1 crop artifact; renders byte-identical); fix-hud-zoom-tilt's premise
(superseded by merged fix-viewmodel-zoom); weapon-matrix3's repair rows (superseded by matrix4/4b);
walkreal-c's "16 door[7] stays 0x2" symptom (stale); fix-level-chain's old blocker table (stale parse);
fix-progress-detected-hook's "port uses fixed values" premise (resolved by 110d); the old fix-menus/pause
page-identity guesses (settled by parsed page data); the Sentinel 0.0625 ride-wait framing (superseded by the
measured 0.33666); "striped boxes wrongly hidden" (refined: every hidden object is an invisible volume, the
0x10 gate verified per kind); fix-anim-blend's skip-on-empty blame (that guard exists only on an unmerged
branch); the 110c2 requeue (redundant, 110c landed).

## Session planner: level and spot index

Every open row names its spot inside HOW (`--bond x,y,z,yaw,pitch` for renders, the coordinates/address for the PCSX2 check). This index groups the ledger rows by level so one PCSX2 session can clear a whole column; `r` = render-only rows (no game needed, run in nf-view), the rest need the real game. Rows tagged `pcsx2` with no level sit in `-` (any level / boot-level checks).
